local INTRO_TIME = 7

local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")


local b64chars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local b64lookup = {}
for i = 1, 64 do b64lookup[string.byte(b64chars, i)] = i - 1 end

local function decodeBase64(data)
    data = data:gsub("[^%w%+/=]", "")
    local nativeDecode = (crypt and (crypt.base64decode or (crypt.base64 and crypt.base64.decode)))
        or (syn and syn.crypt and syn.crypt.base64 and syn.crypt.base64.decode)
        or base64_decode or (base64 and base64.decode)
    if nativeDecode then
        local ok, res = pcall(nativeDecode, data)
        if ok and type(res) == "string" and #res > 1000 then return res end
    end
    local out = {}
    local count = 0
    for i = 1, #data, 4 do
        local a, b, c, d = string.byte(data, i, i + 3)
        a, b = b64lookup[a], b64lookup[b]
        c = c and b64lookup[c]
        d = d and b64lookup[d]
        local n = a * 262144 + b * 4096 + (c or 0) * 64 + (d or 0)
        local b1 = math.floor(n / 65536)
        local b2 = math.floor(n / 256) % 256
        local b3 = n % 256
        if c and d then
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(b1, b2, b3)
        elseif c then
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(b1, b2)
        else
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(b1)
        end
        count = count + 1
        if count % 15000 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end


-- =====   v8 (   64x36 -  ) =====
local MOSAIC_DATA = {}
local MOSAIC_SEGS, MOSAIC_PER, MOSAIC_ROWS = 5, 20, 72
local mosaicTemplate = nil
local function makeTemplate()
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "AstaMosaic"
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 80)
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.ClipsDescendants = true
    holder.ZIndex = 0
    for r = 0, MOSAIC_ROWS - 1 do
        local rowStr = MOSAIC_DATA[r + 1]
        for k = 0, MOSAIC_SEGS - 1 do
            local kp = {}
            for i = 0, MOSAIC_PER - 1 do
                local p = (k * (MOSAIC_PER - 1) + i) * 6 + 1
                local cr = tonumber(string.sub(rowStr, p, p + 1), 16)
                local cg = tonumber(string.sub(rowStr, p + 2, p + 3), 16)
                local cb = tonumber(string.sub(rowStr, p + 4, p + 5), 16)
                kp[#kp + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / (MOSAIC_PER - 1), Color3.fromRGB(cr, cg, cb))
            end
            local cell = Instance.new("Frame")
            cell.BorderSizePixel = 0
            cell.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            cell.Size = UDim2.new(1 / MOSAIC_SEGS, 1, 1 / MOSAIC_ROWS, 1)
            cell.Position = UDim2.new(k / MOSAIC_SEGS, 0, r / MOSAIC_ROWS, 0)
            cell.ZIndex = 0
            local gr = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            gr.Color = ColorSequence.new(kp)
            gr.Parent = cell
            cell.Parent = holder
        end
    end
    return holder
end
local function buildMosaic(frame, radius)
    if not mosaicTemplate then mosaicTemplate = makeTemplate() end
    local holder = mosaicTemplate:Clone()
    local hc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    hc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    hc.Parent = holder
    holder.Parent = frame
    local ver = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ver.Name = "AstaVer"
    ver.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 12)
    ver.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 1, -14)
    ver.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ver.Text = "v9"
    ver.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    ver.TextSize = 10
    ver.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    ver.ZIndex = 8
    ver.Parent = frame
end
local function addMosaic(frame, radius, shadeTransparency)
    if true then return end
    if frame:FindFirstChild("AstaMosaic") then return end
    local ok, err = pcall(buildMosaic, frame, radius)
    if not ok then
        warn("Asta mosaic error: " .. tostring(err))
        local e = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        e.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 40)
        e.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -44)
        e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        e.TextWrapped = true
        e.TextSize = 9
        e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
        e.Text = tostring(err)
        e.ZIndex = 9
        e.Parent = frame
    end
end
pcall(addMosaic, langFrame, 10, 0.8)

    while not astaBgAsset and not astaBgFailed and waited < 6 and not introDone do
        task.wait(0.1)
        waited = waited + 0.1
    end

    tweenService:Create(introImage, TweenInfo.new(1.2), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()
    tweenService:Create(introImage, TweenInfo.new(INTRO_TIME, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {Size = UDim2.new(1.4, 0, 1.4, 0)}):Play()
end)

for i = 1, 32 do
    local flake = Instance.new("Frame")
    local s = math.random(3, 7)
    local x = math.random(0, 100) / 100
    flake.Size = UDim2.new(0, s, 0, s)
    flake.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, -0.05, 0)
    flake.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    flake.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(2, 6) / 10
    flake.BorderSizePixel = 0
    flake.ZIndex = 3
    flake.Parent = introFrame
    local fc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    fc.Parent = flake
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(math.random() * 4)
        while not introDone and flake.Parent do
            flake.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, -0.05, 0)
            local tw = tweenService:Create(flake, TweenInfo.new(math.random(40, 80) / 10, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                Position = UDim2.new(math.clamp(x + (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15, 0, 1), 0, 1.05, 0)
            })
            tw:Play()
            tw.Completed:Wait()
        end
    end)
end

local function addAstaBackground(frame, radius, shadeTransparency)
    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Name = "AstaBackground"
    img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    img.ZIndex = 0
    img.Parent = frame

    local imgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    imgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    imgCorner.Parent = img

    pcall(addMosaic, frame, radius, shadeTransparency)

    local shade = Instance.new("Frame")
    shade.Name = "AstaShade"
    shade.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    shade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    shade.BackgroundTransparency = shadeTransparency
    shade.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shade.ZIndex = 2
    shade.Parent = frame

    local shadeCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    shadeCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
    shadeCorner.Parent = shade

    task.spawn(function()
        local waited = 0
        while not astaBgAsset and not astaBgFailed and waited < 15 do
            task.wait(0.1)
            waited = waited + 0.1
        end
        if frame.Parent then
            addMosaic(frame, radius, shadeTransparency)
        end
    end)
end

function createKeySystem()
    keyFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    keyFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 290, 0, 160)
    keyFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -145, 0.4, -80)
    keyFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
    keyFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    keyFrame.Active = true
    keyFrame.Draggable = true
    keyFrame.Parent = screenGui

    local keyCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    keyCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    keyCorner.Parent = keyFrame

    -- =====      =====
    keyFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    keyFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    local luxGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    luxGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(58, 18, 110)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(22, 12, 58)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(88, 52, 14))
    })
    luxGrad.Rotation = 45
    luxGrad.Parent = keyFrame

    local goldStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    goldStroke.Thickness = 2
    goldStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    goldStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    goldStroke.Parent = keyFrame
    local goldGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    goldGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 120)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(184, 134, 11)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 120))
    })
    goldGrad.Parent = goldStroke
    task.spawn(function()
        while keyFrame and keyFrame.Parent do
            goldGrad.Rotation = (goldGrad.Rotation + 2) % 360
            luxGrad.Rotation = 45 + math.sin(os.clock()) * 15
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
    end)

    local goldLine = Instance.new("Frame")
    goldLine.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 2)
    goldLine.Position = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 0, 40)
    goldLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    goldLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    goldLine.ZIndex = 3
    goldLine.Parent = keyFrame
    local lineGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    lineGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(184, 134, 11)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 120)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(184, 134, 11))
    })
    lineGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    lineGrad.Parent = goldLine

    local titleLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    titleLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleLabel.Text = "\216\167\216\179\216\170\216\167 \226\157\164\239\184\143"
    titleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    titleLabel.TextSize = 16
    titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    titleLabel.ZIndex = 3
    titleLabel.Parent = keyFrame

    task.spawn(function()
        while keyFrame and keyFrame.Parent do
            for i = 0, 1, 0.005 do
                if not keyFrame or not keyFrame.Parent then break end
                titleLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(i, 1, 1)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end
    end)

    local textBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    textBox.Size = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 0, 38)
    textBox.Position = UDim2.new(0.075, 0, 0, 52)
    textBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 8, 34)
    textBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 150, 110)
    textBox.PlaceholderText = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\167\216\175\216\174\217\132 \216\167\217\132\217\133\217\129\216\170\216\167\216\173 \217\135\217\134\216\167" or "Enter key here"
    textBox.Text = ""
    textBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    textBox.TextSize = 14
    textBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    textBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    textBox.ZIndex = 3
    textBox.Parent = keyFrame
    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxStroke.Thickness = 1.2
    boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(212, 175, 55)
    boxStroke.Transparency = 0.25
    boxStroke.Parent = textBox

    local boxCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    boxCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    boxCorner.Parent = textBox

    local submitBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    submitBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 0, 35)
    submitBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.075, 0, 0, 100)
    submitBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    submitBtn.Text = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\170\216\173\217\130\217\130 \217\133\217\134 \216\167\217\132\217\133\217\129\216\170\216\167\216\173" or "Submit Key"
    submitBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 15, 5)
    submitBtn.TextSize = 14
    submitBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    submitBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    submitBtn.ZIndex = 3
    submitBtn.Parent = keyFrame
    local btnGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    btnGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 232, 130)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(212, 165, 32)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(150, 105, 10))
    })
    btnGrad.Rotation = 90
    btnGrad.Parent = submitBtn

    local subCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    subCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    subCorner.Parent = submitBtn

    submitBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if textBox.Text == "mmkkmm" then
            keyFrame:Destroy()
            showSuccessNotification()
            autoFrame.Visible = true
        else
            textBox.Text = ""
            textBox.PlaceholderText = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\167\217\132\217\133\217\129\216\170\216\167\216\173 \216\174\216\183\216\163! \216\173\216\167\217\136\217\132 \217\133\216\172\216\175\216\175\216\167\217\139" or "Wrong Key! Try again"
        end
    end)
end

function showSuccessNotification()
    local notif = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    notif.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 45)
    notif.Position = UDim2.new(1, -255, 1, -65)
    notif.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
    notif.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    notif.Text = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\170\217\133 \216\170\217\129\216\185\217\138\217\132 \216\167\217\132\216\179\217\131\216\177\216\168\216\170\n\216\170\217\133 \216\170\217\129\216\185\217\138\217\132 \216\167\217\132\217\134\216\184\216\167\217\133 \216\168\217\134\216\172\216\167\216\173" or "Script Activated\nSystem activated successfully"
    notif.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    notif.TextSize = 13
    notif.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    notif.Parent = screenGui

    local nc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    nc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    nc.Parent = notif

    local closeX = Instance.new("TextButton")
    closeX.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
    closeX.Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 4)
    closeX.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    closeX.Text = "\226\156\149"
    closeX.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
    closeX.TextSize = 12
    closeX.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    closeX.Parent = notif

    closeX.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() notif:Destroy() end)
    task.delay(3, function()
        if notif and notif.Parent then notif:Destroy() end
    end)
end

local function mkFrame(x, y, w, h, color, z)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
    f.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = color
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.ZIndex = z
    f.Parent = scene
    return f
end

local stars = {}
for i = 1, 16 do
    local sz = math.random(1, 2)
    local st = mkFrame(math.random(4, 204), math.random(3, 44), sz, sz, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1)
    stars[i] = {obj = st, ph = math.random() * 6.28, sp = 1 + math.random() * 2}
end

local function peak(cx, cy, size, z, baseColor, capColor)
    local d = Instance.new("Frame")
    d.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
    d.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx - size / 2, 0, cy - size / 2)
    d.Rotation = 45
    d.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    d.BorderSizePixel = 0
    d.ZIndex = z
    d.Parent = scene
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, capColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, capColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.42, baseColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, baseColor)
    })
    g.Rotation = 45
    g.Parent = d
    return d
end

--  (  )
peak(155, 92, 64, 1, Color3.fromRGB(95, 140, 205), Color3.fromRGB(235, 246, 255))
peak(48, 96, 74, 1, Color3.fromRGB(70, 115, 190), Color3.fromRGB(245, 250, 255))
peak(105, 104, 52, 1, Color3.fromRGB(110, 155, 215), Color3.fromRGB(240, 248, 255))

local function hill(cx, cy, r, z, c1, c2)
    local h = Instance.new("Frame")
    h.Size = UDim2.new(0, r * 2, 0, r * 2)
    h.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx - r, 0, cy - r)
    h.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    h.BorderSizePixel = 0
    h.ZIndex = z
    h.Parent = scene
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0)
    c.Parent = h
    local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2)
    g.Rotation = 90
    g.Parent = h
end
hill(28, 162, 75, 2, Color3.fromRGB(245, 251, 255), Color3.fromRGB(190, 215, 240))
hill(158, 168, 72, 2, Color3.fromRGB(240, 249, 255), Color3.fromRGB(185, 212, 240))
hill(98, 182, 76, 3, Color3.fromRGB(252, 254, 255), Color3.fromRGB(200, 224, 245))

local function pine(x, baseY, scale, z)
    local trunk = mkFrame(x - 1, baseY - 3, 3, 5, Color3.fromRGB(70, 50, 40), z)
    local sizes = {13, 11, 9}
    local ys = {baseY - 9, baseY - 15, baseY - 21}
    for i = 1, 3 do
        local sz = sizes[i] * scale
        peak(x, ys[i] * 1 - (scale - 1) * 6, sz, z, Color3.fromRGB(28, 82, 110), Color3.fromRGB(240, 250, 255))
    end
end
pine(12, 99, 1, 3)
pine(28, 102, 1.15, 3)
pine(178, 100, 1.1, 3)
pine(194, 97, 1, 3)
pine(150, 104, 0.9, 3)

local snowHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
snowHolder.Name = "AstaSnow"
snowHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
snowHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
snowHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
snowHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
snowHolder.ZIndex = 2
snowHolder.Parent = autoFrame

local SNOW_COUNT = 34
local flakes = {}
for i = 1, SNOW_COUNT do
    local size = math.random(3, 7)
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    f.BackgroundTransparency = math.random(0, 4) / 10
    f.BorderSizePixel = 0
    f.ZIndex = 2
    f.Parent = snowHolder
    local fc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    fc.Parent = f
    flakes[i] = {
        obj = f,
        x = math.random() ,
        y = math.random() * 1.2 - 0.1,
        speed = 0.12 + math.random() * 0.25 + size * 0.02,
        sway = 0.01 + math.random() * 0.03,
        phase = math.random() * 6.28,
        swaySpeed = 0.8 + math.random() * 1.5
    }
end

--
task.spawn(function()
    local last = os.clock()
    while autoFrame and autoFrame.Parent do
        local now = os.clock()
        local dt = math.min(now - last, 0.1)
        last = now
        if autoFrame.Visible then
            for _, fl in ipairs(flakes) do
                fl.y = fl.y + fl.speed * dt
                if fl.y > 1.05 then
                    fl.y = -0.08
                    fl.x = math.random()
                end
                local sx = fl.x + math.sin(now * fl.swaySpeed + fl.phase) * fl.sway
                fl.obj.Position = UDim2.new(sx, 0, fl.y, 0)
            end
            auroraGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(now * 0.5) * 0.35, 0)
            for _, st in ipairs(stars) do
                st.obj.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15 + (math.sin(now * st.sp + st.ph) + 1) * 0.35
            end
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end)

local autoActive = false

    autoActive = not autoActive
    if autoActive then
        toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 180, 0)
        toggleBtn.Text = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\167\217\132\216\167\216\179\217\138\216\179\216\170: \216\180\216\186\216\167\217\132" or "Assist: ON"
    else
        toggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 0)
        toggleBtn.Text = currentLang == "ar" and "\216\167\217\132\216\167\216\179\217\138\216\179\216\170: \217\133\216\170\217\136\217\130\217\129" or "Assist: OFF"
    end
end)


--        ( /   / )
task.spawn(function()
    while screenGui and screenGui.Parent do
        if langFrame and langFrame.Parent and not langFrame:FindFirstChild("AstaMosaic") then
            pcall(addMosaic, langFrame, 10, 0.8)
        end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- =================    =================
local lockBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
lockBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 24)
lockBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 5)
lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 45)
lockBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
lockBtn.Text = "\240\159\148\147"
lockBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
lockBtn.TextSize = 14
lockBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
lockBtn.ZIndex = 6
lockBtn.Parent = autoFrame

local lockCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
lockCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
lockCorner.Parent = lockBtn

local frameLocked = false
lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    frameLocked = not frameLocked
    autoFrame.Draggable = not frameLocked
    lockBtn.Text = frameLocked and "\240\159\148\146" or "\240\159\148\147"
    lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = frameLocked and Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 45)
end)
-- ====================================================


runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not autoActive then return end

    local character = localPlayer.Character
    if not character then return end

    local hasTool = false
    for _, item in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if item:IsA("Tool") then
            hasTool = true
            break
        end
    end
    if not hasTool then return end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not rootPart then return end

    local nearestTarget = nil
    local shortestDist = math.huge

    for _, p in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= localPlayer and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local targetHumanoid = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (rootPart.Position - p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < shortestDist then
                    shortestDist = dist
                    nearestTarget = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if nearestTarget then
        humanoid:MoveTo(nearestTarget.Position)
    end
end)
